Regression: register_lookup.json golden comparison on every CI push.

17.6 Pitfalls

Pitfall Manifestation Root Cause Debug signal Recovery
Stale prefix entry (backend evicted KV, not yet notified) Route to backend that re-runs prefill Eviction notification lag prefix_stale_hit_total counter (backend returns cache_miss in response headers) Backend reports X-Cache-Miss: prefix_hash; infr-dispatch triggers eviction
Prefix hash collision (Blake3 truncation) Two distinct prompts hash to same PrefixHash Probability < 2^-128; negligible N/A Use full 32-byte Blake3; no truncation
Tokenizer mismatch (router vs backend) Prefix boundary computed at wrong token Router uses different tokenizer than model backend prefix_match_depth anomalies vs expected Embed tokenizer config in ModelId metadata; validate on backend registration
RadixNode edge splitting bug Incorrect longest-match (returns shorter match) Off-by-one in edge comparison Unit test: fixture longest_match_sequence.jsonl Fuzz test with random token sequences + brute-force oracle
Write lock contention under high registration rate Registration latency spike Post-completion callbacks flood channel; batch writer blocks prefix_registration_latency_us p99 spike Increase coalesce window; or use sharded PrefixTree by ModelId
prompt_tokens field large (64k token prompt) Memory spike in Request struct Arc<[u32]> for full prompt request_prompt_tokens_size histogram Truncate to first K tokens before storing; K = 256 default
Backend set grows unbounded per prefix Memory OOM Many backends register same prefix; no eviction prefix_tree_size gauge growth Cap backends per PrefixEntry at max_backends_per_prefix (default 16); LRU evict oldest

17.7 Performance Concerns

  • RadixNode HashMap children: HashMap<u32, ...> per node; for branching factor < 4 (common: single continuation), use SmallVec<[(u32, ...); 2]> to avoid allocation.
  • Write lock scope: batch registration reduces lock acquisitions from N/request to N/batch. Target: ≤1 write lock per 10 ms window.
  • longest_match read path: acquires read() lock; O(L) traversal; L=256 tokens → ~256 node descents. Each descent = HashMap::get + pointer follow. Expect 1–2 µs in practice.
  • PrefixHash storage: 32 bytes; stored in PrefixEntry and as RadixNode.hash. Total per-entry: ~32 + 4*N_backends bytes. 10k entries, 4 backends avg → ~1.4 MB.
  • Arc<[u32]> for prompt_tokens: shared across Request clone in routing/dispatch; no copy. Drop after routing decision if not needed for registration.
  • Background TTL sweep: O(N) walk of all entries; at 10k entries, ~10 µs. Run infrequently (60 s interval); no impact on routing.
  • Tokenization at ingress: BPE tokenizer CPU cost ~50–200 µs for 256 tokens. Run on tokio::task::spawn_blocking if CPU-bound; do not block async executor.

17.8 Edge Cases

Case Handling
prompt_tokens length < prefix granularity (e.g., 3 tokens) Register short prefix; valid; longest_match may return it for other short prompts
Same PrefixHash registered from 2 backends simultaneously Both writes merge into SmallVec; DashMap-style: last write wins per (hash, backend) pair
Backend removed from registry (not just ejected) infr-registry sends BackendRemoved event; PrefixTree calls evict_all_for_backend(id)
PrefixTree write lock poisoned (panic in writer) PoisonError on write(); treat as InferError::Internal; reset tree to empty
Prefix registered for model A, request is model B longest_match is called with model-scoped tree or ModelId prefix on key; no cross-model contamination
Token IDs non-deterministic (sampling-based tokenizer) Same text → different hashes on re-run
Request with stream = false Prefix registration still occurs on completion; no difference

17.9 Failure Analysis

Failure Detection Blast radius Containment Recovery
PrefixTree memory leak (eviction not running) prefix_tree_size gauge grows monotonically Memory growth → OOM eventually Alert on prefix_tree_size > 100k; TTL sweep auto-runs Restart background sweep task; operator can POST /admin/prefix-tree/flush
Prefix routing causes load imbalance One backend receives 90%+ traffic (popular prefix) That backend overloads; others idle candidate_count = 1 in routing metrics; kv_util spike Blend score: PrefixHit backend score = 0.5*prefix_score + 0.5*load_score; spill to 2nd backend if load > threshold
Registration channel overflow Post-completion registrations dropped; cache-hit rate degrades High request rate + slow batch writer prefix_registration_drop_total counter Increase channel capacity; tune coalesce window
RadixNode tree corruption (bug) Incorrect routing; detected by integration test Wrong backend selection; no safety violation (Health invariant still holds) Fallback: disable prefix routing via feature flag; use LeastLoad only Bug fix + tree rebuild (flush + re-register from recent completions)

Postmortem: prefix_hit_total / prefix_miss_total ratio over time reveals eviction lag; prefix_match_depth histogram shows whether tree is populated.

17.10 Operational Notes

  • Metrics: infr_route_prefix_hit_total{model}, infr_route_prefix_miss_total{model}, infr_route_prefix_tree_size{model} (gauge), infr_route_prefix_match_depth_tokens{model} (histogram), infr_route_prefix_registration_latency_us (histogram), infr_route_prefix_eviction_total{model,reason} (counter).
  • Tracing: tracing::debug! on PrefixHit decisions (include hash, match_depth, backends); tracing::info! on tree flush/eviction batch.
  • Admin endpoints: GET /admin/prefix-tree?model=X → JSON { node_count, entry_count, top_prefixes: [{hash, backends, last_seen}] }.
  • Feature flag: route_config.prefix_routing_enabled: bool (default true); disable without restart via config reload.
  • Deployment: PrefixTree is in-memory; cold-start has 0 entries → all requests use LeastLoad until warmed. Warm-up time = prefix_ttl (10 min default). Document: canary deploy will have lower cache-hit rate during warm-up.
  • Rollback: disable prefix_routing_enabled; routing degrades to LeastLoad; no data loss.
  • Compatibility: Request.prefix_hash and prompt_tokens are new fields (Phase 5). infr-dispatch and infr-queue ignore unknown fields; backward-compatible.
  • Alerting: warn if prefix_hit_total / (prefix_hit_total + prefix_miss_total) < 0.3 under shared-prefix workload (tree not warming properly).

18. Phase 6 — Admission control + queueing — per-model bounded queues, priority, SLO deadlines, load shedding, backpressure

18.1 Objectives

  • Implement infr-queue crate: per-model bounded MPSC queues, priority-ordered scheduling, SLO deadline tracking, load shedding, backpressure signal emission.
  • Constraints introduced: every admitted request holds exactly one Lease; queue saturation MUST NOT cause unbounded memory growth (bounded-queue invariant).
  • Scope: sits between infr-ingress and infr-route; receives Request, emits (Request, Lease) downstream, emits QueueSignal to infr-sched.
  • Out of scope: global fairness quotas (Phase 9 scheduler), per-user billing gates (auth out of scope).
Goal Target
Per-model queue max depth configurable; default 2048
Shed latency (reject + respond) < 500 µs p99
Priority inversion prevention strict via priority-ordered deque
SLO deadline expired in queue shed before dispatch
Backpressure signal emitted to ingress when queue ≥ 80% full
Memory ceiling queue depth × sizeof(Request) ≤ configured cap

18.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

Anchors: P1 (backpressure propagated — I4 bounded-queue invariant), P6 (bounded resource), I4 (bounded-queue), I8 (SLO deadline admission gate), ADR-13 (tokio watch for backpressure signal), B8 (SRE — shed policy design), B3 (Art of Multiprocessor Programming — BinaryHeap concurrency)

Key types: PrioritizedEntry{deadline,priority,request} (Ord: priority desc, then deadline asc within tier); QueueManager{queues: DashMap<ModelId,ModelQueue>, config}; ModelQueue{heap: Mutex<BinaryHeap<PrioritizedEntry>>, slots: AtomicU32, max_depth}; QueueConfig{max_depth, shed_policy, backpressure_threshold, slo_slack_ms}; BackpressureState{model, utilization, shed_rate}.

File Responsibility
lib.rs Public API: QueueManager, QueueConfig, QueueSignal, admit(), dequeue()
model_queue.rs ModelQueue: BinaryHeap<PrioritizedEntry> + slot guard
deadline.rs DeadlineChecker: tokio::time::interval(10ms) background task per ModelQueue; drains expired entries, emits shed events, decrements slots; graceful shutdown drains remainder via oneshot
shed.rs ShedPolicy: Tail (reject) | HeadOldest (evict lowest-priority, refund slot) | Probabilistic(f32)
backpressure.rs BackpressureSender: watch::Sender<BackpressureState>; infr-ingress holds watch::Receiver per model → 429/RESOURCE_EXHAUSTED with x-queue-util
lease.rs Lease creation + drop-guard: impl Drop decrements slots if backend still None; assigned leases completed explicitly via Lease::complete()/fail()
metrics.rs Per-model queue_depth, shed_total, deadline_expired_total gauges

admit(): resolve ModelId → if slots ≥ max_depth apply ShedPolicy → if slo.deadline already expired, shed (DeadlineExceeded) → slots.fetch_add(1, Release) + heap push → emit QueueSignal::Enqueued → if slots/max_depth ≥ backpressure_threshold, send BackpressureState → return Lease{request_id, backend:None, deadline, drop_guard}.

dequeue() (called by infr-route): lock heap, pop+discard expired entries (incrementing deadline_expired_total) until a live one is found → pop highest-priority → slots.fetch_sub(1, Release) → activate Lease.backend once routing decides → return (request, lease).

Observability: queue_admitted_total{model}, queue_depth{model} gauge, queue_shed_total{model,reason} (full_tail\|full_head\|probabilistic\|deadline_expired); span queue.admit{model_id,priority,deadline_ms,queue_depth}.

Test harness: test_bounded_no_oom (fill to max_depth → QueueFull), test_priority_order (mixed priority, descending dequeue), test_deadline_shed (past-deadline discarded, no Lease), test_backpressure_signal (80% fill → watch update), test_lease_drop_refunds_slot (drop without complete → slot decrements), proptest (random priority/deadline, slots never exceed max_depth).

18.3 Deliverable

  • infr-queue crate compiles; QueueManager::admit + dequeue pass all unit tests.
  • infr-ingress integrates backpressure watch; returns HTTP 429 with x-queue-util header when triggered.
  • cargo test -p infr-queue green; clippy clean.
  • Grafana dashboard panel: queue_depth{model=...} gauge + queue_shed_total rate visible in local dev.

18.4 Acceptance Criteria

Criterion Threshold
test_bounded_no_oom passes; RSS stable under 10 MB for 100k enqueue/reject cycles
test_priority_order 1000-item mixed-priority dequeue; zero inversions
test_deadline_shed 100% expired entries shed before dispatch; no leaked Leases
Shed latency (reject path) p99 < 500 µs on 10k concurrent shed requests (criterion bench)
Backpressure signal latency watch update seen by ingress within 1 ms of threshold crossing
Slot accounting proptest 10k runs: slots ∈ [0, max_depth] always
No Lease leak valgrind/ASAN or miri: zero dropped Lease without slot release

18.5 Conformance Fixtures

File Purpose Expected Output
fixtures/queue/admit_normal.json 100 requests, mixed priority, all within SLO 100 Leases issued; queue_depth gauge == 100
fixtures/queue/shed_full_tail.json 2048+1 requests, tail policy request 2049 → QueueFull; queue_depth == 2048
fixtures/queue/shed_deadline_expired.json Requests with deadline < now all shed; deadline_expired_total == N
fixtures/queue/priority_inversion_probe.json Low-pri enqueued first, high-pri second dequeue order: high first
fixtures/queue/backpressure_trigger.json Fill to 82% of max_depth BackpressureState.utilization ≥ 0.80 emitted
fixtures/queue/lease_drop_no_complete.json Enqueue + drop Lease without complete slots returns to 0; no panic
fixtures/queue/malformed_no_model.json Request with empty ModelId InferError::InvalidRequest; no queue mutation
fixtures/queue/replay_deterministic.json Replay same seed; compare shed counts shed counts identical (deterministic shed policy)

Regression: re-run all fixtures after any ShedPolicy or PrioritizedEntry::Ord change.

18.6 Pitfalls

  • Priority inversion under mixed ShedPolicy: head-oldest eviction pops lowest-priority entry from heap but may evict an entry that was close to its deadline, causing silent SLO violation for that evicted request. Detection: track shed_reason=head_evict + correlate with deadline_expired_total spike. Fix: evict by (priority ASC, deadline ASC) not just priority.
  • Slot counter drift on panic: if admit panics between slots.fetch_add and heap push, slot leaks. Root cause: non-atomic admit sequence. Fix: use RAII SlotGuard { counter: &AtomicU32 } that decrements on Drop; upgrade to heap-push only on guard commit().
  • Deadline sweeper starvation: 10ms interval may lag under high load if tokio runtime is starved. Manifestation: expired entries linger in heap, dispatch receives them, backend sees stale deadline. Instrumentation: sweeper_lag_ms histogram. Recovery: run sweeper on dedicated tokio::runtime::Builder::new_current_thread or increase interval priority.
  • DashMap shard contention: QueueManager uses DashMap<ModelId, ModelQueue>. With 1 model, all admits contend on 1 shard. Root cause: small model cardinality. Fix: pre-allocate DashMap::with_capacity_and_shard_amount(16, 64) or replace with HashMap<ModelId, Arc<ModelQueue>> + per-model lock.
  • Backpressure hysteresis oscillation: threshold at 80% → signal emitted; ingress throttles → queue drops to 60% → signal cleared; ingress floods again. Manifestation: oscillating 429 rate. Fix: hysteresis band (on at 80%, off at 60%); expose BackpressureState { on_threshold, off_threshold }.
  • watch channel lag: tokio::sync::watch delivers only the latest value; rapid queue fill + drain between poll intervals means ingress misses transient pressure. Instrumentation: backpressure_signal_missed_total counter (compare expected vs received events). Mitigation: combine watch with a Semaphore-based hard cap at ingress.
  • Lease double-release race: dispatch calls Lease::complete() while tokio timeout fires Lease::drop() concurrently. Root cause: missing atomic state flag. Fix: AtomicBool released guard inside Lease; complete() and drop() both CAS from false → true; only winner decrements slot.

18.7 Performance Concerns

  • Heap lock contention: BinaryHeap under Mutex is a single-threaded bottleneck per model. Mitigation: sharded by request ID's low bits for sub-model parallelism (future); or use lock-free priority queue (crossbeam-skiplist keyed by (priority_neg, deadline_ns)).
  • Allocation per entry: PrioritizedEntry heap-allocates Request (which contains prompt_hash: PrefixHash = 32 bytes). Prefer Box<Request> stored in a pool slab (slab crate) to amortize allocator pressure at high QPS.
  • fetch_add / fetch_sub false sharing: AtomicU32 slots on same cache line as heap pointer → store slots on a separate #[repr(align(64))] struct field.
  • Deadline sweep interval: 10ms sweeper holds lock during drain; under pathological all-expired queue, drain is O(N). Bound drain iteration to 256 entries per sweep tick; emit sweep_truncated metric if heap still non-empty after bound.
  • Backpressure watch serialization: watch::Sender::send() acquires a lock per call. Throttle to at most 1 send per 5ms per model using Instant-gated debounce.
  • BinaryHeap reallocation: pre-allocate BinaryHeap::with_capacity(max_depth) at QueueManager init to avoid growth reallocs.

18.8 Edge Cases

  • max_depth = 0: all admits shed immediately; dequeue returns None. Assert in config validation.
  • deadline = Instant::now(): request is already expired at enqueue time; shed in admit before touching heap.
  • priority wrap: if Priority is u8, adding new tiers risks ordering inversion. Use enum Priority { Low=0, Normal=1, High=2, Critical=3 } with explicit discriminants; never rely on numeric arithmetic.
  • Model removed while queue non-empty: infr-registry fires ModelRemoved event. Handler must drain queue: send InferError::ModelUnavailable to all waiting Leases before dropping ModelQueue.
  • Concurrent reconfigure (max_depth change): live resize must not lose in-flight entries. Approach: compare-and-swap on max_depth atomic; if shrinking, refuse new admits until slots < new_max.
  • Clock skew between ingress and queue: if ingress and queue run on different logical clocks, deadline comparison may be wrong. Enforce: deadlines are Instant (monotonic, process-local) set at ingress parse time; not wall-clock.
  • Zero-length prompt_tokens: valid for continuation requests; queue must not divide by zero in any slot-size calculation.
  • Starvation of low-priority requests: under sustained high-priority load, low-priority entries never dequeue. Mitigation: age-based priority promotion (bump priority by 1 tier after configurable wait_ms); emit starvation_promoted_total.

18.9 Failure Analysis

Failure Detection Blast Radius Containment Recovery
slots leak (counter > actual heap size) slots > queue_depth_actual divergence gauge Reduces effective capacity; future admits incorrectly rejected Alert on divergence > 5 Restart infr-queue process; drain queue
Deadline sweeper panic sweeper task absent from tokio::task::JoinSet Stale expired entries accumulate; dispatch sees dead requests Sweeper is supervised; restart on panic Auto-restart via JoinSet; emit sweeper_restart_total
DashMap deadlock (nested lock) Span latency spike on queue.admit > 100ms All admits for affected model block Never lock DashMap entry while holding ModelQueue lock Fix call ordering; add lock ordering doc comment
Backpressure watch channel closed watch::Sender::send() returns Err Ingress no longer throttles; queue overflows Check return; if closed, disable backpressure path gracefully Reconnect watch pair on config reload
Heap corruption (unsafe code) Segfault / ASAN Full process crash Use safe BinaryHeap; never unsafe in queue path Run miri in CI; all queue code must be #[forbid(unsafe_code)]

Postmortem guidance: on QueueFull rate spike, check queue_depth gauge (sustained max?), shed_policy config, downstream backend_latency_p99 (slow backends cause queue buildup), and autoscale signal lag (Phase 9).

18.10 Operational Notes

  • Metrics: queue_depth{model} (gauge), queue_admitted_total{model} (counter), queue_shed_total{model,reason} (counter), queue_deadline_expired_total{model} (counter), queue_lease_active{model} (gauge), queue_backpressure_state{model} (gauge 0/1).
  • Tracing: span queue.admit tagged model_id, priority, slo_deadline_ms, queue_util; span queue.dequeue tagged wait_ms (time between admit and dequeue).
  • Structured logs: INFO queue_shed reason=full_tail model=<id> depth=2048; WARN queue_deadline_expired model=<id> count=N lag_ms=M.
  • Config hot-reload: QueueConfig fields max_depth, backpressure_threshold, slo_slack_ms reloadable via infr-cli reload; no restart required. shed_policy change drains in-flight gracefully.
  • Deployment: infr-queue is a library crate embedded in the router binary; no separate process. Queue state is ephemeral (in-memory); on router restart all queued requests shed with InferError::RouterRestart (clients retry).
  • Migration: when increasing max_depth, no migration needed (additive). When decreasing: emit warning if current slots > new_max; block new admits until drained.
  • Rollback: canary deployment; watch queue_shed_total rate; rollback if shed rate > 5× baseline within 5 minutes.

19. Phase 7 — Dispatch + connection pooling — multiplexed HTTP/2 conns to backends, streaming token relay, flow control, timeouts

19.1 Objectives

  • Implement infr-dispatch crate: per-backend multiplexed HTTP/2 connection pool, request forwarding, streaming token relay (SSE/chunked/gRPC-stream), flow control, per-request timeouts (TTFT + total), circuit-breaker stubs (full circuit breaker in Phase 8).
  • Constraints: order invariant (streaming relay preserves backend token order byte-for-byte); at-most-once invariant (non-idempotent requests sent to exactly one backend); Lease released on completion/failure.
  • Scope: consumes (Request, Lease, RoutingDecision) from infr-route; returns token stream to infr-ingress.
  • Not in scope: retry/hedge logic (Phase 8), circuit-breaker state machine (Phase 8).
Goal Target
Connection pool per backend min 2, max 64 H2 streams/conn; configurable
TTFT timeout per-SLO slo.ttft_ms; default 10 s
Total request timeout per-SLO slo.deadline; hard ceiling 300 s
Streaming relay overhead < 50 µs added per token chunk (p99)
Pool connection reuse > 95% requests reuse existing H2 stream
Graceful drain in-flight streams complete before pool close

19.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

Anchors: ADR-15 (hyper-util connection pool), I3 (at-most-once — Lease prevents double-dispatch), I5 (Lease invariant — released on drop), I10 (stream order), B7 (Computer Networking — H2 flow-control window), P8 (Tail at Scale — TTFT timeout budget), B2 (DDIA — idempotency and at-most-once)

File Responsibility
lib.rs dispatch(decision, request, lease, client_tx) -> JoinHandle<Result<(),DispatchError>>: look up BackendPool by decision.backend; acquire PooledConn; build HTTP/2 request; spawn relay_task; arm TtftGuard/TotalGuard; on completion call Lease::complete()/fail(err)
pool.rs BackendPool{conns: Arc<Mutex<VecDeque<PooledConn>>>, semaphore, config: PoolConfig{min_conns,max_conns,max_streams_per_conn,idle_timeout,connect_timeout}}; acquire = semaphore + pop healthy conn or open new H2 conn; release = push back or close if idle; H2 settings INITIAL_WINDOW_SIZE=4MB, MAX_CONCURRENT_STREAMS=128; TLS via hyper-rustls, optional cert pinning
conn.rs PooledConn: H2 SendRequest wrapper; tracks last_used: Instant; PING if idle >5s to detect half-open TCP
relay.rs StreamRelay{response: h2::RecvStream, client_tx, ttft_guard}: loop response.data().await → forward Bytes to client_tx; first chunk disarms TtftGuard + records ttft_ms; flow_control().release_capacity(n) after every chunk (prevents H2 recv-window deadlock); SSE lines re-emitted verbatim (no buffering/reparse); gRPC frames passed through unmodified; order invariant enforced — no reordering buffer
timeout.rs TtftGuard = sleep(ttft_deadline) → cancel relay + TtftTimeout; TotalGuard = sleep_until(slo.deadline) → abort + TotalTimeout; both composed via tokio::select!; outgoing request carries x-inference-deadline header
metrics.rs Per-backend conn_active, stream_active, bytes_relayed, timeout_total
error.rs DispatchError wrapping InferError variants

Flow control: client_tx bounded at 64 chunks; slow client → H2 send window fills → backend naturally rate-limited; if full > flow_control_timeout_ms → abort relay, InferError::ClientSlow.

Observability: dispatch_ttft_ms{backend,model}/dispatch_total_ms{backend,model} histograms; dispatch_timeout_total{backend,kind} (ttft\|total\|client_slow); pool_conn_active{backend}/pool_stream_active{backend} gauges; span dispatch.relay (child of route.decide) with backend_id,ttft_ms,chunks,bytes.

Test harness: mock backend (wiremock/axum, chunked SSE); test_streaming_order (1000 tokens, exact order), test_ttft_timeout (15s delay → timeout at 10s), test_total_timeout (slow stream past deadline), test_pool_reuse (100 sequential reqs → pool_conn_active ≤ 2), test_flow_control_release (slow client, no H2 deadlock), test_client_slow_abort (stalled client_tx → abort within flow_control_timeout_ms+10ms).

19.3 Deliverable

  • infr-dispatch crate compiles; all dispatch tests pass.
  • End-to-end: infr-ingress → infr-queue → infr-route → infr-dispatch → mock backend; streaming SSE response received correctly at client.
  • Connection pool metrics visible in Prometheus scrape: pool_conn_active, pool_stream_active.
  • cargo test -p infr-dispatch green; no H2 window stalls in 10-min load test.

19.4 Acceptance Criteria

Criterion Threshold
test_streaming_order 1000 tokens received in exact order; zero reorderings
test_ttft_timeout Timeout fires within ttft_ms ± 50ms; InferError::TtftTimeout returned
test_total_timeout Deadline enforced; relay aborts; Lease released
Pool reuse rate ≥ 95% in test_pool_reuse (100 requests, 2 conns)
Relay overhead p99 < 50 µs per chunk (criterion bench with 64-byte chunks)
H2 window deadlock zero deadlocks in 10-min chaos test (slow client + fast backend)
Lease released on timeout proptest: every dispatch path (ok/timeout/error) releases Lease exactly once

19.5 Conformance Fixtures

File Purpose Expected Output
fixtures/dispatch/sse_stream_100_tokens.jsonl 100 SSE events from backend Client receives 100 events, exact text match
fixtures/dispatch/ttft_delay_12s.json Backend first token at 12s; SLO ttft=10s TtftTimeout at 10s ± 50ms
fixtures/dispatch/total_deadline_300s.json Stream ongoing past 300s deadline TotalTimeout; stream terminated
fixtures/dispatch/malformed_h2_frame.bin Invalid H2 frame bytes from backend DispatchError::ProtocolError; conn evicted from pool
fixtures/dispatch/client_slow_stall.json Client recv paused 30s ClientSlow abort within flow_control_timeout_ms
fixtures/dispatch/grpc_streaming.bin gRPC server-streaming response Length-prefix frames re-emitted correctly
fixtures/dispatch/conn_pool_exhaustion.json 65+ concurrent requests, pool max=64 65th request queues on semaphore; no panic
fixtures/dispatch/graceful_drain.json Shutdown signal during active stream In-flight stream completes; no truncation

19.6 Pitfalls

  • H2 recv window exhaustion (deadlock): relay reads chunk, forwards to client, but neglects release_capacity(n). Backend's send window fills → backend blocks → relay blocks → deadlock. Detection: dispatch_ttft_ms spike without backend CPU activity. Fix: always call flow_control().release_capacity(n) immediately after consuming each chunk.
  • Pooled connection half-open after backend restart: TCP connection appears alive (OS-level), but backend has reset. First request on that conn fails with RST. Manifestation: ~1% error rate correlated with backend restarts. Detection: pool_conn_error_total counter spike. Fix: PING before reuse after idle > 5s; on RST, evict conn, retry on new conn (idempotent only).
  • Lease not released on relay task panic: if relay_task panics, Lease::complete() / fail() never called. Root cause: panic unwind bypasses explicit calls. Fix: wrap relay body in AssertUnwindSafe + catch_unwind; call Lease::fail in catch block.
  • SSE re-framing breaks chunk boundaries: backend sends multi-event chunks (e.g., data: tok1\ndata: tok2\n\n in one H2 DATA frame). Relay must preserve raw bytes, not split. Invariant: relay is a byte-pipe, not a parser. Manifestation: client SSE parser misaligns events. Fix: never re-parse SSE in relay; forward Bytes verbatim.
  • gRPC length-prefix double-wrap: if relay adds its own chunked encoding on top of gRPC frames, client receives garbled responses. Root cause: content-type negotiation mismatch. Fix: detect content-type: application/grpc and disable additional framing.
  • Semaphore::acquire starvation under burst: pool semaphore acquisition is FIFO in tokio; burst of 64 concurrent requests exhausts semaphore; 65th waits indefinitely if pool never releases. Mitigation: semaphore acquisition timeout = min(slo.deadline - now, 5s); on timeout emit InferError::PoolExhausted.
  • TLS cert expiry silent failure: rustls returns connection error; pool silently retries and exhausts retries; requests fail with opaque error. Instrumentation: log cert expiry in PooledConn::new; alert if cert expires < 7 days.

19.7 Performance Concerns

  • Chunk copy on relay: Bytes from H2 RecvStream is zero-copy (backed by H2 recv buffer); forwarding to client_tx avoids allocation if client_tx is Sender<Bytes> (not Vec<u8>). Never .to_vec() in hot path.
  • Semaphore acquisition overhead: tokio::sync::Semaphore::acquire is lock-free but involves an atomic CAS + possible wakeup; at 50k req/s, cost is ~200ns/request. Acceptable.
  • H2 header compression (HPACK): per-request headers (model, adapter, deadline) decoded/encoded each request. Pre-encode static headers (model, path) once per pool.
  • Relay task spawn overhead: tokio::spawn per request = ~300ns. At 10k req/s negligible; at 100k req/s → 30ms/s CPU. Alternative: reuse a pinned relay loop per connection (future optimization; don't prematurely optimize in Phase 7).
  • Mutex<VecDeque> pool contention: under high concurrency, pool lock becomes bottleneck. Mitigation: sharded pool (one VecDeque per CPU shard, indexed by request_id % shards).
  • TCP send buffer saturation: large token streams (10k tokens × 4 bytes) = 40KB/stream. At 1000 concurrent streams = 40MB in flight. Ensure SO_SNDBUF tuned (32KB min); monitor dispatch_bytes_in_flight gauge.

19.8 Edge Cases

  • Backend sends empty stream (0 tokens): TtftGuard fires after ttft_ms unless disabled for empty-response models. Config: PoolConfig.allow_empty_stream: bool.
  • Backend sends HTTP 503 mid-stream (after headers, before body): relay receives error in body stream. Treat as DispatchError::BackendError(503); do not relay partial tokens to client.
  • Connection count == 0 during cold start: pool has no conns; first request must open conn within connect_timeout. If backend unreachable → DispatchError::ConnectTimeout; no infinite wait.
  • request.max_tokens = 0: backend may return immediately (empty output) or error. Relay should handle 0-chunk response gracefully.
  • Router shutdown during relay: tokio::CancellationToken broadcast to all relay tasks; each task drains current chunk, closes stream, releases Lease. No partial writes to client.
  • Backend sends duplicate stream IDs (H2 protocol violation): h2 crate returns h2::Error; log and evict conn.
  • Overflow in chunks_sent counter: use u64; at 1M tokens/stream × 1M concurrent streams = 10^12; u64 sufficient.
  • SLO deadline set to UNIX_EPOCH (zero): treat as "no deadline" if deadline == Instant::MIN; otherwise enforce normally.

19.9 Failure Analysis

Failure Detection Blast Radius Containment Recovery
Backend crashes mid-stream H2 RST or TCP RST during relay All in-flight requests to that backend fail Circuit-breaker stub (Phase 8) + pool eviction Route pending requests to healthy replicas
Pool leak (conn never returned) pool_conn_active grows unbounded No new requests served for that backend Timeout on conn acquisition; evict leaked conn pool.audit() on health check; force evict idle > 60s
Relay task leak (never completes) pool_stream_active grows; dispatch_total_ms histogram tail Memory growth; Lease never released → slot leak Hard timeout TotalGuard (300s max) JoinSet monitors relay tasks; abort on join timeout
H2 GOAWAY from backend h2::Error::GoAway All streams on that conn terminated Drain conn; open fresh conn; retry idempotent only Log GOAWAY reason; emit conn_goaway_total
TLS handshake storm All new conns fail; connect_timeout spike All new requests to backend fail Rate-limit new conn opens: max 10/s per backend Backoff + alert on cert issues

Postmortem: on dispatch timeout spike, check backend_latency_p99 (backend overloaded?), pool_conn_active (pool exhausted?), flow_control_stall_total (client slow?).

19.10 Operational Notes

  • Metrics: dispatch_ttft_ms{backend,model} hist, dispatch_total_ms{backend,model} hist, dispatch_timeout_total{backend,kind} counter, pool_conn_active{backend} gauge, pool_stream_active{backend} gauge, dispatch_bytes_relayed_total{backend} counter, dispatch_error_total{backend,reason} counter.
  • Tracing: span dispatch.acquire_conn (pool wait), dispatch.send_request, dispatch.relay_stream; parent = route.decide. Export to OTLP.
  • Structured logs: INFO dispatch_complete backend=B req=R ttft_ms=120 total_ms=3400 chunks=512; WARN dispatch_timeout kind=ttft backend=B slo_ms=10000.
  • Config hot-reload: PoolConfig.max_conns, idle_timeout, connect_timeout hot-reloadable; pool resizes lazily (no conn eviction on shrink, just refuse new conns above new max).
  • Deployment: pool per backend initialized on first use (lazy); pre-warm via Dispatcher::warmup(backend_id, min_conns) called from infr-health on replica registration.
  • Drain procedure: on SIGTERM, CancellationToken broadcast; relay tasks complete current chunk; pool closes idle conns; infr-ingress stops accepting new requests. Timeout: 30s before force-kill.
  • Compatibility: backends must speak HTTP/2 + OpenAI-compatible /v1/completions or /v1/chat/completions with stream=true; gRPC backends must expose standard inference proto.

20. Phase 8 — Resilience — retries (idempotency-aware), request hedging, circuit breakers, outlier ejection, deadline propagation

20.1 Objectives

  • Implement full resilience layer in infr-dispatch (retry/hedge/circuit-break) and infr-health (outlier ejection): retries only for idempotent=true requests; hedging only for idempotent=true; circuit breakers per backend preventing cascade; outlier ejection for latency/error outliers; deadline propagation on every outbound request.
  • Invariants reinforced: at-most-once (non-idempotent never retried/hedged); health invariant (Ejected backend never dispatched).
  • Constraints: hedged request duplication must be cancelled on first response; circuit-breaker state must persist across config reloads (in-memory only).
  • Out of scope: global retry budgets across requests (future); retrying non-idempotent requests (never).
Goal Target
Retry eligibility request.idempotent == true only
Max retry attempts configurable; default 2 (3 total)
Hedge trigger p99 latency exceeded by hedge_percentile_ms
Circuit-breaker open threshold ≥ 50% error rate over 10s window
Ejection threshold latency p99 > 2× fleet median; or error rate > 20%
Ejection recovery re-admit after ejection_interval_ms (default 30s)
Deadline propagation x-inference-deadline header on every backend request

20.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

Anchors: P8 (Tail at Scale — hedging design), I3 (at-most-once — hedge gated on idempotent: bool), ADR-04 (Envoy outlier detection FSM — R4 reference), I11 (circuit breaker per BackendId), ADR-15 (connection pool with circuit-breaker wrapper), B6 (Envoy Proxy Deep Dive — circuit breaker)

File Responsibility
infr-dispatch/retry.rs RetryPolicy{max_attempts,backoff_base_ms,jitter,retryable_errors}; should_retry(req,err,attempt): false if !idempotent or attempt≥max_attempts; true for ConnectTimeout|BackendUnavailable|H2GoAway; false for TtftTimeout|TotalTimeout|ClientSlow; backoff = min(base×2^attempt + jitter(0..base), remaining_deadline_ms), skipped if it would exceed deadline; retry re-routes via infr-route (fresh RoutingDecision), old Lease released first
infr-dispatch/hedge.rs HedgePolicy{enabled,hedge_after_ms,max_hedges}, eligible only if idempotent==true: dispatch primary, arm hedge_timer; on fire (no response yet) dispatch secondary to a different backend; first response cancels the other via CancellationToken (cancelled Lease::fail(HedgeCancelled) frees its slot); both fail → return first error; hedge_after_ms defaults to 2×p50 from infr-health EWMA; metrics hedge_fired_total{model}, hedge_won_total{backend}
infr-dispatch/circuit.rs Per-backend FSM Closed→Open→HalfOpen→Closed; CircuitBreaker{state: AtomicU8, error_count, success_count, window_start, config: CbConfig{error_threshold_pct,window_ms,half_open_max,open_duration_ms}}; Closed trips to Open at errors/total ≥ threshold_pct; Open rejects with CircuitOpen until open_duration_ms then HalfOpen; HalfOpen admits half_open_max probes, all-succeed→Closed, any-fail→Open; Dispatcher::dispatch() checks allow() before pool acquire; state is in-memory only (resets on restart)
infr-dispatch/deadline.rs propagate_deadline(): inserts x-inference-deadline (from slo.deadline) and x-inference-request-id headers before every outbound H2 request; compliant backends self-cancel past deadline
infr-health/ejection.rs OutlierEjector{backends: DashMap<BackendId,EjectionState{latency_p99_ms,error_rate,ejected_at,consecutive_fails}>}; trigger (per probe cycle, 10s): latency_p99 > 2.0×fleet_median OR error_rate > threshold, gated by consecutive_fails≥3; recovery: after ejection_interval_ms → Degraded, one passing probe → Healthy; pushes Health to infr-registry (routing enforces the invariant, not dispatch); emits ejection_total{backend}, ejection_recovery_total{backend}

Test harness: test_retry_idempotent_success (503 then success), test_retry_non_idempotent_no_retry, test_retry_deadline_exhausted, test_hedge_first_wins (slow primary, fast hedge cancels primary), test_circuit_opens_on_error_rate, test_circuit_half_open_recovery, test_ejection_outlier_latency (3×median → Ejected), test_deadline_header_propagated.

20.3 Deliverable

  • infr-dispatch + infr-health crates compile with resilience modules; all resilience tests pass.
  • Circuit breaker state visible in GET /debug/circuits endpoint (JSON: backend → state).
  • Ejection events visible in structured logs + ejection_total metric.
  • Chaos test: 30% backend error injection; p99 latency improvement vs no-resilience baseline measured.

20.4 Acceptance Criteria

Criterion Threshold
test_retry_non_idempotent_no_retry passes; at-most-once invariant never violated
test_hedge_first_wins hedge cancels primary within 10ms of hedge response; zero duplicate responses to client
Circuit opens on ≥ 50% errors opens within window_ms of threshold crossing
Circuit half-open recovery recovers on half_open_max consecutive successes; no false positives
Ejection latency outlier triggers within 2 probe cycles of threshold violation
Retry deadline-aware zero retries attempted when backoff + latency_est > remaining_deadline
Resilience overhead retry/hedge control path adds < 1 ms p99 to non-error requests
Chaos test (30% error injection) p99 TTFT degrades < 20% vs baseline; error rate seen by clients < 2%

20.5 Conformance Fixtures

File Purpose Expected Output
fixtures/resilience/retry_idempotent_503.json Idempotent req; backend 1 returns 503; backend 2 succeeds Single success response; retry_total=1
fixtures/resilience/no_retry_non_idempotent.json Non-idempotent req; backend 503 Error returned immediately; retry_total=0
fixtures/resilience/hedge_fast_backend_wins.json 2 backends: slow + fast; hedge_after_ms=500 Fast backend response returned; slow cancelled
fixtures/resilience/circuit_open_sequence.json 20 consecutive errors from backend Circuit opens after error_threshold_pct exceeded
fixtures/resilience/circuit_halfopen_recovery.json Circuit open; 3 probe successes Circuit transitions Closed; circuit_state{backend}=0
fixtures/resilience/ejection_latency_spike.json Backend latency_p99 = 3× median Health::Ejected set; ejection_total += 1
fixtures/resilience/deadline_header_presence.json Backend echoes request headers x-inference-deadline and x-inference-request-id present
fixtures/resilience/retry_deadline_expired.json Retry backoff > remaining deadline Zero retry attempts; InferError::DeadlineExceeded
fixtures/resilience/hedge_both_fail.json Both primary and hedge fail Error returned; two Lease::fail calls; zero duplicate responses

20.6 Pitfalls

  • Retry amplification (thundering herd): all retried requests simultaneously hit the same backends after a transient outage clears. Root cause: fixed backoff with no jitter. Fix: mandatory jitter (rand::thread_rng().gen_range(0..backoff_base_ms) added to backoff); cap at remaining deadline.
  • Hedge duplicate response race: primary and hedge both complete nearly simultaneously; relay sends two responses to client channel. Root cause: cancellation not instantaneous (tokio task not yet polled). Fix: AtomicBool response_sent CAS in relay; only first winner sets it; second drops its response bytes.
  • Circuit breaker split-brain under replicas: each router replica maintains independent circuit state. One replica may have circuit open while another has closed. Root cause: no shared state. Acceptable: circuit breakers are advisory; health ejection (shared via infr-registry) is the authoritative ban. Document this explicitly.
  • Half-open probe starvation: half_open_max = 1; single probe request times out; circuit re-opens; no recovery possible if backends recover but probes always pick the one slow path. Fix: set half_open_max ≥ 3; probe with minimum-size requests; use RouteReason::CircuitProbe to distinguish in metrics.
  • Ejection flapping: backend oscillates between healthy and ejected (marginal backend). Manifestation: ejection_total increments rapidly. Root cause: consecutive_fails threshold too low or probe interval too short. Fix: consecutive_fails ≥ 3 before eject; exponential backoff on re-probe after ejection (first re-probe at 30s, then 60s, 120s).
  • x-inference-deadline clock skew: router and backend on different NTP sources; backend sees deadline already past. Root cause: wall-clock usage. Mitigation: add x-inference-remaining-ms header (relative) alongside absolute deadline; backend uses relative if absolute seems stale.
  • Non-retriable errors miscategorized: InferError::BackendError(500) from a model crash vs. BackendError(500) from a prompt that always fails (deterministic). Retrying deterministic failures wastes resources. Fix: backends should use distinct status codes (503 = transient, 422 = deterministic bad input); only retry 503/H2 errors.
  • Lease double-release on hedge cancel: hedge cancel path calls Lease::fail(HedgeCancelled) from cancellation handler; if relay task also calls Lease::fail concurrently, slot released twice. Fix: AtomicBool released guard in Lease; fail() is idempotent.

20.7 Performance Concerns

  • Hedge doubling of backend load: every hedged request consumes 2× backend slots. Under load, hedging can push backends into overload, worsening the latency that triggered hedging. Mitigation: HedgePolicy.max_fleet_hedge_rate: f32 (default 0.05); disable hedging if fleet-wide hedge rate > threshold. Emit hedge_suppressed_total.
  • Circuit-breaker atomic contention: error_count, success_count updated on every request completion. At 100k req/s, CAS contention on single backend circuit. Mitigation: per-CPU shard counters (like Prometheus counters); aggregate periodically.
  • Retry re-routing overhead: retry path invokes infr-route::decide() again; re-acquires infr-registry read lock, re-evaluates PrefixTree. Cost: ~5µs. Negligible for 2 retries; acceptable.
  • Ejection probe frequency: OutlierEjector runs every 10s; iterates all backends computing p99. At 1000 backends, cost is O(1000 × histogram_read) ≈ 1ms. Acceptable on dedicated background task.
  • Backoff timer accuracy: tokio::time::sleep(backoff_duration) resolution ≈ 1ms. Backoff values < 1ms lose precision. Minimum backoff: 5ms.
  • AtomicU8 FSM false sharing: circuit breaker state: AtomicU8 packed with counters on same cache line → false sharing on high-throughput. Pad FSM state to #[repr(align(64))].

20.8 Edge Cases

  • All backends ejected simultaneously: fleet-wide outage. infr-route has no healthy backend to route to; returns InferError::NoHealthyBackend. Queue continues to accumulate; infr-sched emits autoscale signal. Do NOT retry indefinitely; fail fast with clear error.
  • Retry with different adapter on fallback backend: retry selects a backend that doesn't have the required AdapterId. infr-route must enforce adapter availability during retry re-routing; emit InferError::AdapterUnavailable if no alternative.
  • idempotent=true set incorrectly by client: router trusts request.idempotent; if wrong, duplicate requests reach backend. Documentation must warn: clients set idempotent=true only for truly idempotent completions (e.g., exact repeat prompts with deterministic sampling). Router cannot verify this.
  • Circuit window_ms shorter than backend request p99: all requests time out within window; circuit opens spuriously. Fix: window_ms should be ≥ 5× backend p99 TTFT. Config validation: warn if window_ms < 5 × ewma_ttft_ms.
  • Hedge fires on streaming request: streaming requests (request.stream=true) are long-lived; hedging means two streams open to client. Semantically undefined. Fix: disable hedging for stream=true requests.
  • Ejector with 1 backend in fleet: 2× fleet_median ejection triggers with only 1 backend → that single backend gets ejected → no healthy backends. Fix: min_healthy_backends config; ejection skipped if would leave fewer than min_healthy.
  • open_duration_ms = 0 (config error): circuit opens and immediately enters HalfOpen, never providing recovery isolation. Config validation: open_duration_ms ≥ 1000ms.
  • Concurrent retry + hedge: request eligible for both retry and hedge. Policy: hedge fires first (if primary slow); if hedge also fails, retry on fresh backend. Never hedge a retry. HedgeTask not created for retry attempts.

20.9 Failure Analysis

Failure Detection Blast Radius Containment Recovery
Circuit open storm (all backends trip simultaneously) circuit_open_total{backend=*} spike; NoHealthyBackend error rate Full outage for affected model Shed all new requests; emit autoscale signal Circuit HalfOpen probes; manual POST /admin/circuit/reset endpoint
Hedge storm (hedge rate > 5%) hedge_fired_total / request_total > 0.05 2× backend load → cascade Auto-suppress hedging via max_fleet_hedge_rate Reduce hedge_after_ms or disable hedging; investigate backend p99
Retry budget exhausted (all retries fail) retry_exhausted_total counter; client error rate spike Degraded throughput; clients see errors Max retry cap (2) limits blast; circuit breaker opens if persistent Investigate backend health; check ejection_state
Ejection flapping ejection_total > 2/min for same backend Routing instability; cache affinity lost Backoff on re-probe; consecutive_fails ≥ 3 gate Fix marginal backend; or permanently remove from fleet
Hedge Lease double-release lease_active gauge goes negative Slot accounting corrupted; future admits miscounted AtomicBool released guard prevents Restart router shard; re-count slots from scratch
Deadline propagation missing (header not set) Backend processes past deadline; wasted GPU cycles (not a client-visible error) Wasted compute; tail latency increase Alert if x-inference-deadline absent in > 1% of backend requests (sampled via tracing) Fix propagate_deadline() call site

Postmortem triggers: any circuit_open_total event should auto-create incident; on-call checks ejection_state, backend_error_rate, recent deploys.

20.10 Operational Notes

  • Metrics: retry_total{model,backend,attempt} counter, retry_exhausted_total{model} counter, hedge_fired_total{model} counter, hedge_won_total{backend} counter, hedge_suppressed_total{model} counter, circuit_state{backend} gauge (0=Closed,1=HalfOpen,2=Open), circuit_open_total{backend} counter, ejection_total{backend} counter, ejection_active{backend} gauge, ejection_recovery_total{backend} counter.
  • Tracing: span dispatch.retry (attempt number); span dispatch.hedge (primary vs secondary); span health.ejection_check; all as children of root request span.
  • Structured logs: WARN circuit_opened backend=B error_rate=0.62 window_ms=10000; INFO circuit_recovered backend=B; WARN ejection_triggered backend=B latency_p99_ms=4500 fleet_median_ms=1200; INFO ejection_recovery backend=B.
  • Admin endpoints (served by infr-cli/router debug HTTP):
    • GET /debug/circuits → JSON map backend → {state, error_rate, last_opened_at}.
    • POST /admin/circuit/reset?backend=B → force circuit to Closed (emergency override; audit logged).
    • GET /debug/ejections → JSON map backend → {ejected, ejected_at, consecutive_fails}.
    • POST /admin/ejection/restore?backend=B → force Health::Healthy (with warning).
  • Config hot-reload: RetryPolicy, HedgePolicy, CbConfig, EjectionConfig all hot-reloadable; circuit breaker state preserved across reload.
  • Deployment notes: resilience parameters (thresholds, windows) must be tuned per-model based on production TTFT distributions; default values are conservative. Deploy with hedge_policy.enabled=false initially; enable after baseline latency distribution is known.
  • Rollback: if resilience changes cause unexpected circuit flapping, roll back CbConfig via hot-reload (no restart); if retry amplification observed, set max_attempts=0 via reload to disable retries immediately.

21. Phase 9 — Adapter-aware + multi-model routing — LoRA adapter inventory, route to replicas with adapter resident, adapter swap-cost awareness

21.1 Objectives

  • Adapter inventory: infr-registry tracks per-BackendId loaded AdapterId set; exposes query replicas_with_adapter(model, adapter) -> Vec<BackendId>.
  • Adapter-aware routing: infr-route selects only replicas where adapters.contains(adapter_id) before any load policy; RouteReason::adapter_resident.
  • Fallback chain: adapter_resident → prefix_hit on compatible replica → least_load with swap allowed → shed if no replica can serve.
  • Swap-cost model: each replica emits adapter_swap_cost_ms: f32 in LoadState; routing penalizes replicas requiring a swap proportional to this cost.
  • Multi-model fanout: infr-route enforces model == replica.model hard filter before all other policies; ModelId mismatch is never dispatched.
  • Constraints introduced: Request.adapter is Option<AdapterId>; None means base model only (no adapter filter). Swap-cost is advisory, not a hard block.
  • Scope: infr-registry adapter CRUD, infr-route adapter filter, infr-health adapter-swap latency probe, infr-telemetry adapter-hit/swap metrics.

21.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

Anchors: I1 (single-writer registry — adapter inventory update is atomic), I7 (adapter inventory — no dispatch to backend lacking requested adapter), ADR-14 (ArcSwap for wait-free adapter inventory reads), P4 (DistServe — adapter placement model), ADR-16 (PrefixHash extended to adapter namespace)

Crate / File Responsibility
infr-registry/adapter_inventory.rs AdapterInventory{DashMap<BackendId,HashSet<AdapterId>>, RwLock<HashMap<AdapterId,AdapterMeta{id,model,size_gb,load_time_ms_p50}>>}; register_adapter()/evict_adapter() (notified by health probe or config reload); replicas_with_adapter(model,adapter) -> Vec<BackendId> (O(replicas)); adapter_swap_cost(backend) -> f32 (0.0 if resident)
infr-health/adapter_probe.rs Per health tick: scrape /v1/adapters → HashSet<AdapterId> per backend; diff → register/evict; populate LoadState.adapter_swap_cost_ms from /v1/adapters/swap_cost or AdapterMeta.load_time_ms_p50
infr-route/adapter_filter.rs Pipeline: model_filter (hard reject mismatched ModelId) → adapter_filter (keep adapter-resident only, if req.adapter.is_some()) → prefix_score (PrefixTree longest-match) → swap_penalized_load_score(r) = ewma_ttft_ms + adapter_swap_cost_ms×SWAP_WEIGHT (default 1.5) → min-score select. Empty adapter-resident set + allow_adapter_swap=true → fallback to model-matching replicas, RouteReason::adapter_swap; empty fallback → InferError::NoCapableReplica
infr-queue No structural change — Request.adapter passes through unchanged
infr-sched/adapter_balance.rs Tracks adapter_load: HashMap<AdapterId,u32> (inflight per adapter); emits autoscale hint when saturated with no idle adapter-resident replica
infr-telemetry Counters route_reason{adapter_resident|adapter_swap}, adapter_swap_total; histograms adapter_swap_cost_ms, adapter_routing_latency_us; gauge adapter_residents{adapter_id}

Test harness: adapter_routing_bench.rs (4 replicas/3 adapters, 10k tagged requests, ≥95% adapter-resident hit); chaos (evict mid-run → fallback activates, no panic, RouteReason shifts).

21.3 Deliverable

  • infr-registry exposes replicas_with_adapter and adapter_swap_cost; hot-reload from config.
  • infr-route enforces adapter filter; emits RouteReason::adapter_resident | adapter_swap | no_capable_replica.
  • infr-health scrapes and diffs adapter inventory each tick.
  • infr-telemetry exports adapter_swap_total, adapter_swap_cost_ms histogram.
  • Operator can query GET /debug/adapters → JSON: backend → loaded adapters, swap cost.

21.4 Acceptance Criteria

Check Threshold
adapter_routing_bench adapter-resident hit rate ≥ 95% under steady load
Adapter filter pass latency (p99) ≤ 50 µs
Fallback to swap path on eviction activates within 1 health tick (≤5s)
route_reason{adapter_resident} counter increments verified by unit test
No request dispatched to wrong-model replica 0 violations in 100k-request integration test
InferError::NoCapableReplica on zero-replica case asserted in unit test
adapter_swap_cost_ms histogram populated p50 > 0 when swap occurs

21.6 Pitfalls

Pitfall Manifestation Root Cause Debug Signal Recovery
Stale adapter inventory requests routed to replica that evicted adapter; backend returns 404 health probe missed eviction event adapter_swap_total spike; 404 rate in backend error log reduce health tick interval; add reactive eviction webhook
Swap-cost starvation high-swap replicas never selected even when idle SWAP_WEIGHT too high, all replicas carry swap cost adapter_swap_cost_ms histogram flat at 0; route_reason=no_capable_replica lower SWAP_WEIGHT; ensure adapter pre-loading
Multi-model routing bypass base-model request dispatched to fine-tuned replica model_filter using string prefix match instead of exact ModelId wrong model in response; model_mismatch_total counter enforce exact ModelId equality; add invariant assert
Adapter inventory race adapter registered before replica ready health probe races with replica startup request dispatched during loading → backend 503 add adapter_ready: bool field; only route when ready
Cold-start all replicas lack adapter 100% InferError::NoCapableReplica on deploy adapter not pre-loaded on any replica adapter_residents{a}=0 gauge pre-load adapter before traffic; use canary gate
DashMap contention under high eviction rate lock spin on AdapterInventory frequent concurrent evict+register high CPU on routing thread; adapter_inventory_lock_wait_us histogram shard by BackendId; or switch to epoch-based update

21.7 Performance Concerns

  • adapter_filter is O(replicas × adapters_per_replica); for 100 replicas × 50 adapters → 5k comparisons; use HashSet::contains (O(1)) not linear scan.
  • DashMap shard count: default 16 shards; tune to 2 × num_cpus to reduce contention at high eviction rates.
  • Avoid cloning HashSet<AdapterId> in hot path; hold read lock for duration of filter, release before dispatch.
  • adapter_swap_cost_ms is read from LoadState (stack-allocated f32); no allocation in scoring loop.
  • Telemetry counter increments: use RelaxedCounter (atomic u64, SeqCst only at flush) to avoid cache-line ping-pong.
  • Routing decision path (model_filter → adapter_filter → prefix_score → score_min): profile with criterion; budget ≤ 100 µs end-to-end at p99.
  • replicas_with_adapter called per request; cache result for 1 tick window under stable inventory to amortize DashMap reads.

21.8 Edge Cases

  • req.adapter = None on a replica set where all replicas carry adapters: route succeeds (base model serveable); no adapter filter applied.
  • Adapter eviction during in-flight request: request already dispatched; backend handles gracefully; next request re-routes. Lease holds through completion.
  • Two adapters same name, different models: AdapterId must be scoped by ModelId to prevent collision; enforce in AdapterMeta constructor.
  • Replica reports adapter in inventory but OOM on load: backend returns 503; infr-dispatch triggers retry on different replica; adapter_swap_fail_total counter.
  • All replicas ejected + requested adapter only on ejected set: InferError::NoCapableReplica with Health::Ejected context in error payload.
  • Config reload removes adapter entirely: in-flight requests complete; new requests get NoCapableReplica; operator must pre-load replacement.
  • SWAP_WEIGHT = 0.0 edge: adapter filter still enforced; penalty disabled only, not filter.

21.9 Failure Analysis

Failure Detection Blast Radius Containment Recovery
Adapter inventory diverged from actual backend state 4xx/5xx spike on adapter requests; health scrape latency spike all requests for that adapter shed to swap path; alert on adapter_inventory_divergence_total force full re-scrape on next tick; health tick retry
replicas_with_adapter returns empty (all ejected) InferError::NoCapableReplica all requests for this adapter/model shed with 503; preserve queue restore replica health; operator page
Model-filter bug dispatches to wrong model corrupted response; model_id in response != requested all affected model's requests hard assert in dispatch layer; reject non-matching model response hotfix filter; rollback registry version
AdapterMeta size_gb wrong → underestimates swap cost unexpectedly high TPOT; swap dominates latency requests relying on swap adapter_swap_cost_ms alarm correct AdapterMeta; force re-probe

21.10 Operational Notes

  • GET /debug/adapters — JSON: { backend_id: { adapters: [...], swap_cost_ms: f32 } }.
  • adapter_residents Prometheus gauge per (backend_id, adapter_id): alert adapter_residents < 1 for required adapters.
  • Structured log on every RouteReason::adapter_swap: { request_id, backend_id, adapter_id, swap_cost_ms }.
  • Config hot-reload: adapters_allow_swap: bool and swap_weight: f32 in infr-core::Config; reload via SIGHUP without restart.
  • Deployment: pre-load adapters on all replicas before enabling traffic; use /v1/adapters/load on each backend; verify via adapter_residents gauge before canary shift.
  • Rollback: set adapters_allow_swap: false to force adapter-resident-only routing; traffic only reaches prepared replicas.
  • Migration: adapter IDs are stable ULIDs; adding a new adapter doesn't affect existing routes.

22. Phase 10 — Disaggregated prefill/decode + speculative — separate prefill/decode pools, KV handoff coordination, speculative-decode routing

22.1 Objectives

  • Disaggregation model: route prefill phase to prefill_pool replicas, decode phase to decode_pool replicas; coordinate KV handoff between them.
  • Replica role tagging: Replica.role: ReplicaRole enum { Unified, Prefill, Decode }; registry enforces routing constraints per role.
  • KV handoff protocol: after prefill completes, prefill replica emits KV-cache block IDs; router signals decode replica to pull/receive KV blocks before decode starts.
  • Speculative decode routing: for requests with speculative: bool, route to a draft-model replica first; then verify on target model replica; router orchestrates two-hop dispatch.
  • Constraints introduced: disaggregation is opt-in per model config; Unified replicas handle both phases (default). KV handoff adds latency; must complete before decode begins (invariant).
  • Scope: infr-registry role tagging, infr-route disagg routing, infr-sched KV handoff state machine, infr-dispatch two-hop coordination, infr-telemetry prefill/decode/handoff metrics.
  • Invariant: decode replica never starts until KV handoff ACK received from prefill replica; no partial decode on stale KV state.

22.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

Anchors: P4 (DistServe — disaggregated prefill/decode architecture), P5 (Splitwise — KV handoff latency target ≤10ms), I12 (disaggregated coordination — prefill result not lost), ADR-13 (tokio channels for prefill→decode KV handoff coordination), P6 (Mooncake — KV transfer store model)

Types (infr-core): ReplicaRole{Unified,Prefill,Decode} added to Replica; KvHandoffToken{request_id,prefill_backend,kv_block_ids: Vec<u64>,expires_at}; SpeculativeCtx{draft_backend,target_backend,draft_tokens: Vec<u32>}.

File Responsibility
infr-registry/role_index.rs RoleIndex: HashMap<ReplicaRole,Vec<BackendId>> from replica config, updated on reload; prefill_replicas()/decode_replicas()/unified_replicas(); disaggregation_enabled: bool per model (false → all Unified)
infr-sched/kv_handoff.rs KvHandoffRegistry: DashMap<RequestId,KvHandoffToken> with TTL eviction (30s); state machine PrefillDispatched → PrefillComplete(token) → HandoffInProgress → HandoffAck → DecodeDispatch; record_prefill_complete() (called on prefill response); await_handoff_ack(req_id,timeout) (timeout → KvHandoffTimeout); handoff signal = decode backend /v1/kv_receive?block_ids=...&source=prefill_addr
infr-route/disagg_router.rs If enabled: prefill routed to prefill_replicas (prefix_score+least_load); after HandoffAck, decode routed to decode_replicas (least_load only); RouteReason::disagg_prefill|disagg_decode. Unified: unchanged single-hop. Speculative (req.speculative): draft to fastest low-latency replica → verify on target replica → accepted tokens to client
infr-dispatch/two_hop.rs dispatch_prefill(req) -> KvHandoffToken (no client streaming); handoff call + await; dispatch_decode(req,kv_token) -> TokenStream (relays to client); speculative: dispatch_draft()/dispatch_verify(); circuit breaker covers both hops independently
infr-telemetry Histograms prefill_latency_ms/kv_handoff_latency_ms/decode_latency_ms/speculative_accept_rate; counters kv_handoff_timeout_total/speculative_draft_rejected_total/disagg_prefill_total/disagg_decode_total; gauge kv_handoff_pending

Test harness: disagg_integration_test.rs (mock prefill token + decode ACK → e2e stream), kv_handoff_timeout_test.rs (hung decode → KvHandoffTimeout within deadline), speculative_decode_test.rs (3/5 draft accept → correct stream).

22.3 Deliverable

  • Disaggregated path operational: prefill dispatched to prefill pool, KV handoff coordinated, decode dispatched to decode pool.
  • Speculative decode path: draft → verify two-hop working with acceptance rate metric.
  • kv_handoff_latency_ms histogram exported; GET /debug/disagg shows inflight handoff state.
  • Operators can toggle disaggregation_enabled per model via config hot-reload; traffic shifts within 1 tick.

22.4 Acceptance Criteria

Check Threshold
Disagg integration test (mock backends) passes; e2e token stream correct
kv_handoff_timeout_total on timeout test increments; error returned to client
KV handoff latency p99 (local network mock) ≤ 10 ms
Decode NEVER starts before handoff ACK 0 violations across 10k disagg requests
Speculative accept rate metric populated; matches mock draft acceptance
InferError::KvHandoffTimeout on 5s mock hang returned within deadline ± 100 ms
Unified path unaffected when disagg disabled 0 latency regression; existing tests pass

fixtures/disagg/handoff_timeout.yaml decode backend hangs at KV receive KvHandoffTimeout after deadline

22.6 Pitfalls

Pitfall Manifestation Root Cause Debug Signal Recovery
Decode starts before handoff ACK corrupted tokens; garbled output missing await on await_handoff_ack decode_before_handoff_total counter enforce invariant with Rust type state machine; compile-time guarantee
KV block ID collision across requests wrong KV blocks loaded into decode non-unique block IDs (per-backend local counter) output corruption; kv_block_collision_total scope block IDs by (BackendId, RequestId)
Prefill pool exhausted; decode pool idle high prefill queue depth; decoders idle mismatched pool sizing prefill_queue_depth alarm; decode_queue_depth near 0 dynamic rebalancing signal to autoscaler
Speculative draft token length mismatch verify hop receives wrong context draft model uses different tokenizer speculative_tokenizer_mismatch_total enforce draft/target model tokenizer ID match in config
KvHandoffToken TTL too short under load TTL expiry before decode dispatched high decode queue depth delays dispatch kv_token_expired_total spike adaptive TTL based on decode queue ETA; minimum 60s
Two-hop latency doubles P50 vs unified TTFT regression for disaggregated models handoff RTT adds to critical path kv_handoff_latency_ms histogram; compare unified vs disagg TTFT only enable disagg when prefill >> decode dominates; tune pool ratio
Decode backend OOM on KV receive OOM kill; request lost oversized KV blocks beyond decode GPU memory kv_receive_oom_total; backend health eject enforce KV size limit in handoff token; admit control on KV size

22.7 Performance Concerns

  • KV handoff is on the critical path for TTFT in disaggregated mode; target ≤ 5 ms intra-cluster (RDMA or high-bandwidth interconnect assumed).
  • KvHandoffRegistry uses DashMap; shard count = 32 for high-concurrency; entries are small (Vec<u64> block IDs, typical < 1 KB).
  • Avoid blocking tokio executor thread in await_handoff_ack; use tokio::time::timeout + channel receive; no busy-poll.
  • Speculative two-hop: draft dispatch should use lowest-latency backend; avoid queueing draft behind large batch requests; separate draft queue or priority boost.
  • Vec<u32> draft tokens allocated per speculative request; reuse via pool if speculative traffic > 10% of total.
  • Prefill/decode pool sizing imbalance → queuing on one side while other is idle; emit autoscale signals before saturation (§11).
  • Streaming relay: decode tokens flow as they're produced; no buffering in router; infr-dispatch uses zero-copy relay (tokio copy_bidirectional or manual chunk relay).

22.8 Edge Cases

  • Prefill backend crashes mid-prefill: infr-dispatch detects connection reset; request re-queued to prefill pool; KvHandoffToken never created; decode never triggered.
  • Decode backend crashes after ACK but before all tokens streamed: client receives partial stream; infr-dispatch returns InferError::StreamAborted; not retriable (decode already consumed KV).
  • Request arrives with speculative=true but no draft model configured: fall back to standard single-hop routing; speculative_fallback_total counter.
  • kv_block_ids empty (prefill produced no tokens): valid for echo/empty prompt; decode receives empty KV; decode produces tokens from scratch.
  • Hot-reload disabling disaggregation mid-flight: in-flight disaggregated requests complete on current path; new requests use unified path immediately.
  • Network partition between prefill and decode pools: handoff times out; request fails; operator alert; no data corruption (decode never started).
  • Speculative acceptance rate drops to 0%: all tokens re-generated on target; no performance gain; emit speculative_ineffective metric; auto-disable if rate < 5% for 60s.

22.9 Failure Analysis

Failure Detection Blast Radius Containment Recovery
Prefill pool fully ejected prefill_queue_depth → ∞; InferError::NoCapableReplica all disaggregated model requests shed disagg traffic; fall back to unified if unified pool exists restore prefill replicas; or switch model to unified mode
KV handoff network failure kv_handoff_timeout_total spike requests in handoff state retry decode on next decode replica; re-fetch KV fix network; monitor kv_handoff_latency_ms
KvHandoffRegistry memory leak (TTL bug) kv_handoff_pending gauge grows unbounded router memory exhaustion TTL compaction interval check; alert on > 10k pending fix TTL loop; bump compaction frequency
Speculative draft model unavailable speculative_fallback_total spike speculative-flagged requests only transparent fallback to standard routing restore draft model; no user impact

22.10 Operational Notes

  • GET /debug/disagg → JSON: inflight handoffs {request_id, prefill_backend, decode_backend, state, elapsed_ms}.
  • Metrics: prefill_latency_ms, kv_handoff_latency_ms, decode_latency_ms histograms (p50/p95/p99/p999); kv_handoff_pending gauge; speculative_accept_rate gauge.
  • Tracing: span disagg_prefill, kv_handoff, disagg_decode with request_id attribute; link to parent ingress span.
  • Config: disaggregation_enabled: bool, kv_handoff_timeout_ms: u64 = 5000, kv_token_ttl_s: u64 = 60, speculative_enabled: bool per model in infr-core::ModelConfig.
  • Hot-reload via SIGHUP; disagg toggle takes effect for new requests within one scheduling tick.
  • Deployment: bring up prefill pool first, validate health; bring up decode pool; enable disaggregation_enabled; monitor kv_handoff_latency_ms for first 10 minutes.
  • Rollback: set disaggregation_enabled=false; traffic shifts to unified replicas immediately; no data loss.
  • SLO impact: disaggregated TTFT = prefill_latency + handoff_latency; ensure SLO budget accounts for handoff overhead; recommend separate SLO for disagg vs unified.

23. Phase 11 — Autoscaling signals + fairness — emit scale up/down signals, per-tenant fairness/quotas, rate limiting

23.1 Objectives

  • Autoscale signal emission: infr-sched evaluates queue depth, EWMA load, and SLO miss rate; emits ScaleSignal { model, direction: Up|Down, urgency: f32 } to an external consumer (Kubernetes HPA webhook, custom autoscaler, or operator).
  • Per-tenant fairness: infr-sched enforces weighted fair queuing (WFQ) across tenants (TenantId); each tenant has a Quota { tokens_per_s: f32, burst: u32, weight: f32 }.
  • Rate limiting: infr-queue applies token-bucket rate limiting per (TenantId, ModelId) before admission; shed excess with 429.
  • Quota enforcement: requests exceeding quota are shed or delayed; no starvation of low-weight tenants under high-weight tenant saturation.
  • Constraints introduced: Request gains tenant: TenantId; TenantId required for all requests; anonymous requests map to TenantId::default().
  • Scope: infr-sched signal logic + WFQ, infr-queue token-bucket, infr-core type additions, infr-telemetry per-tenant metrics, infr-cli quota config commands.

23.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

Anchors: P13 (Aperture — autoscale signal design), P1 (Orca — batch_hint iteration-level scheduling), ADR-23 (K8s HPA-compatible signal emit, not direct API call), I4 (bounded-queue — fairness loop enforces per-tenant quota), B5 (Kubernetes in Action — HPA custom metrics), B8 (SRE — quota and fairness)

Types (infr-core): TenantId(Arc<str>) (interned); Quota{tokens_per_s,burst,weight,max_inflight}; ScaleSignal{model,direction: ScaleDirection{Up,Down,Hold},urgency,queue_depth,load_p95,timestamp}.

File Responsibility
infr-queue/rate_limiter.rs TokenBucketMap: DashMap<(TenantId,ModelId),TokenBucket{tokens: AtomicF32,last_refill,rate,burst}>; try_admit() → Admitted|RateLimited(retry_after_ms)|Shed; lazy refill (tokens=min(burst, tokens+rate×elapsed)), atomic CAS consumption (no mutex); RateLimited → 429 + Retry-After, rate_limit_total{tenant}++
infr-sched/fairness.rs WfqScheduler: per-tenant min-heap of (virtual_finish_time, RequestId); vft = max(tenant_vft, global_vft) + request_cost/tenant_weight (request_cost = prompt_tokens+max_tokens); next_request() pops min-heap; QuotaRegistry: HashMap<TenantId,Quota> (hot-reload via watch); max_inflight_check() compares inflight counter to Quota.max_inflight
infr-sched/autoscale.rs Eval loop every 10s per model: inputs queue_depth,ewma_load_p95,slo_miss_rate_1m,inflight_tokens_per_s; scale-up on any threshold breach, scale-down needs all-below for STABLE_WINDOW_S evaluations; hysteresis (60s up-cooldown, 300s down-cooldown); emits via ScaleSignalSink trait (WebhookSink HTTP POST or ChannelSink tokio broadcast)
infr-telemetry Per-tenant counters requests_total/tokens_generated_total/rate_limit_total/quota_exceeded_total; gauges tenant_inflight/tenant_queue_depth/tenant_vft; scale_signal_emitted_total{model,direction}, autoscale_evaluation_latency_us
infr-cli/quota_cmd.rs quota set --tenant --tokens-per-s --burst --weight (PATCH /admin/quotas); quota list; scale-log --model --last

Test harness: fairness_test.rs (3 tenants, weights 1:2:4, saturated queue → throughput ratio ±10%), rate_limit_test.rs (100 req/s quota, 200 req/s sent → ~50% 429 ±5%), autoscale_signal_test.rs (queue_depth=1000 → ScaleSignal{Up} within 2 eval intervals).

23.3 Deliverable

  • Token-bucket rate limiting active per (TenantId, ModelId); 429 returned with Retry-After on excess.
  • WFQ scheduler enforces tenant weights; fairness measurable via tenant_vft gauge.
  • ScaleSignal emitted to WebhookSink or ChannelSink on threshold breach; operator can tail signals.
  • infr-cli quota commands operational; quotas hot-reloadable without restart.
  • Per-tenant Prometheus metrics exported; Grafana dashboard template provided.

23.4 Acceptance Criteria

Check Threshold
WFQ fairness ratio test throughput ratio within ±10% of weight ratio
Rate limit accuracy 429 rate within ±5% of excess over quota
Scale-up signal latency emitted within 2 × eval_interval (≤ 20s) of threshold breach
Scale-down hysteresis no Down signal within 300s of Up signal
rate_limit_total{tenant} counter increments correctly per rejected request
tenant_inflight gauge accurate within ±1 of actual inflight
Quota hot-reload new quota effective within 1s of PATCH; no restart
Anonymous request maps to default tenant verified by unit test; no panic
